## Stage ownership and analytical scope

This run uses the available hosted Work environment because it has verified access to intact Japanese EPUBs, filesystem extraction, model text inspection and Git operations. Sequential source ownership is serialized; separate read-only agents handle inventory, bibliographic/structural reconciliation and independent audits. The integration owner alone updates the mutable map/ledgers. The actual model label/reasoning control is not asserted from product branding. Prose-only methods create no AV-inspection requirement; the five image-based mixed books have a later page-inspection obligation.

1. Complete main-series readings, tranche scorecards and Gen04/Gen05 freezes in order, then the final V16–V17 evidence freeze.
2. Complete main-only monographs, specialist syntheses, four principal reconstructions, party/transfer models, simulation/consistency audit and required evidence/revision routes; freeze their exact baseline before side reading.
3. Test YORI-01, MASK, DUST-06 and DUST-07 under fixed models and scene eligibility, preserving failures and ambiguity. Other supplementary books are not selectively recruited additional holdouts.
4. Complete the remaining principal secondary-novel program and individually identified story/bonus/mixed-book narrative components, with explicit chronology/viewpoint/continuity authority. Preserve a separate ordered or expressly retrospective Dust boundary despite its later volumes' main-model holdout use.
5. Integrate justified, attributable post-validation reconstructions and expanded synthesis without changing prospective history or original test results.

The run's scope is the fixed 41-object inventory and identifiable edition-contained components; future releases are outside it. No source has been excluded merely because image text requires additional work. Canon/continuity ambiguity remains an explicit component-level OPEN decision.
